  const SEED = true;                 // set false to create empty lists only
  const m = location.pathname.match(/^\/(sites|teams)\/[^/]+/);
  const SITE = location.origin + (m ? m[0] : '');
  const API = SITE + '/_api';
  const H = { Accept: 'application/json;odata=nometadata', 'Content-Type': 'application/json;odata=nometadata' };
  const digest = async () => { H['X-RequestDigest'] = (await (await fetch(API + '/contextinfo', { method: 'POST', headers: H })).json()).FormDigestValue; };
  await digest();
  const req = async (u, b, x = {}, method = 'POST') => {
    const r = await fetch(API + u, { method, headers: { ...H, ...x }, body: b ? JSON.stringify(b) : undefined });
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(u + ' → ' + r.status + ' ' + (await r.text()).slice(0, 300));
    return r.status === 204 ? null : r.json().catch(() => null);
  };
  const get = async u => { const r = await fetch(API + u, { headers: H }); return r.ok ? r.json() : null; };
  const MERGE = { 'X-HTTP-Method': 'MERGE', 'IF-MATCH': '*' };
  const log = (...a) => console.log('%c[MDI]', 'color:#00B2A9;font-weight:bold', ...a);
  log('Target site:', SITE);
  let n = 0;
  for (const [name, L] of Object.entries(PKG.schema)) {
    n++; if (n % 6 === 0) await digest();
    const P = `/web/lists/getbytitle('${name}')`;
    let exists = await get(P + '?$select=Id');
    if (!exists) { await req('/web/lists', { Title: name, BaseTemplate: L.base, Description: L.desc || '' }); log('created list', name); }
    else log('list exists', name);
    const have = new Set(((await get(P + '/fields?$select=InternalName,Title')) || { value: [] }).value.flatMap(f => [f.InternalName, f.Title]));   // SharePoint encodes names like Q1 / Job1
    for (const xml of L.fields) {
      const nm = (xml.match(/ Name="([^"]+)"/) || [])[1];
      if (nm && have.has(nm)) continue;
      try { await req(P + '/fields/createfieldasxml', { parameters: { SchemaXml: xml, Options: 8 } }); }
      catch (e) { console.warn('[MDI] field failed', name, nm, e.message); }
    }
    try { await req(P + "/fields/getbyinternalnameortitle('Title')", { Title: L.title.name, Required: !!L.title.req, CustomFormatter: L.title.fmt || '' }, MERGE); } catch (e) { console.warn('[MDI] title', name, e.message); }
    // indexes (SharePoint 5,000-item threshold) + unique keys (Title* = enforce unique)
    for (const c of ((PKG.indexes || {})[name] || [])) {
      const uq = c.endsWith('*'), cn = c.replace('*', '');
      try {
        const f = await get(P + `/fields/getbyinternalnameortitle('${cn}')?$select=Id,Indexed,EnforceUniqueValues`);
        if (f && (!f.Indexed || (uq && !f.EnforceUniqueValues))) { await req(P + `/fields('${f.Id}')`, uq ? { Indexed: true, EnforceUniqueValues: true } : { Indexed: true }, MERGE); log('indexed', name + '.' + cn + (uq ? ' (unique)' : '')); }
      } catch (e) { console.warn('[MDI] index failed', name, cn, e.message); }
    }
    try {
      const v = (await get(P + '/defaultview?$select=Id')).Id, V = P + `/views('${v}')`;
      await req(V + '/viewfields/removeallviewfields');
      for (const f of L.view) { try { await req(V + `/viewfields/addviewfield('${f}')`); } catch (e) {} }
      await req(V, { ViewQuery: L.query || '', RowLimit: L.rows || 100 }, MERGE);
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[MDI] view', name, e.message); }
  }
  // ---- choice values added after first release (merged into existing columns) ----
  for (const [name, fs] of Object.entries(PKG.choiceAdds || {})) {
    for (const [fname, adds] of Object.entries(fs)) {
      try {
        const P = `/web/lists/getbytitle('${name}')/fields/getbyinternalnameortitle('${fname}')`;
        const f = await get(P + '?$select=Id,Choices'); if (!f) continue;
        const have = f.Choices || [], need = adds.filter(a => !have.includes(a));
        if (!need.length) continue;
        await req(`/web/lists/getbytitle('${name}')/fields('${f.Id}')`, { '__metadata': { type: 'SP.FieldChoice' }, Choices: { '__metadata': { type: 'Collection(Edm.String)' }, results: have.concat(need) } },
          { ...MERGE, 'Content-Type': 'application/json;odata=verbose', Accept: 'application/json;odata=verbose' });
        log('choices added', name + '.' + fname, need.join(', '));
      } catch (e) { console.warn('[MDI] choices', name, fname, e.message); }
    }
  }
  if (SEED) {
    await digest();
    for (const [name, rows] of Object.entries(PKG.seed)) {
      const P = `/web/lists/getbytitle('${name}')`;
      const cnt = (await get(P + '?$select=ItemCount')).ItemCount;
      if (cnt > 0) { log('seed skipped (not empty)', name); continue; }
      let i = 0;
      for (const r of rows) {
        const body = {}; for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(r)) if (v !== null && v !== undefined) body[k] = v;
        try { await req(P + '/items', body); i++; } catch (e) { console.warn('[MDI] seed row failed', name, e.message); }
        if (i % 40 === 0) await digest();
      }
      log('seeded', name, i, 'rows');
    }
  }
  // ---- config rows added after first release (only if missing) ----
  for (const [name, rows] of Object.entries(PKG.ensureRows || {})) {
    for (const r of rows) {
      try {
        const ex = await get(`/web/lists/getbytitle('${name}')/items?$select=ID&$filter=Title eq '${r.Title.replace(/'/g, "''")}'`);
        if (ex && ex.value && ex.value.length) continue;
        await req(`/web/lists/getbytitle('${name}')/items`, r); log('added', name, r.Title);
      } catch (e) { console.warn('[MDI] row', name, r.Title, e.message); }
    }
  }
  // ---- permissions: Contribute - no delete, MDI Config Editors, per-list access ----
  const PERMS = true;   // set false to leave list permissions inherited
  if (PERMS && PKG.perms) {
    await digest();

      let rd = ((await get("/web/roledefinitions?$filter=Name eq 'Contribute - no delete'&$select=Id")) || { value: [] }).value[0];
      if (!rd) {
        const c = await get("/web/roledefinitions/getbyname('Contribute')?$select=BasePermissions");
        const low = (BigInt(c.BasePermissions.Low) & ~BigInt(0x88)).toString();   // remove DeleteListItems + DeleteVersions
        const r = await fetch(API + '/web/roledefinitions', { method: 'POST', headers: { ...H, 'Content-Type': 'application/json;odata=verbose', Accept: 'application/json;odata=verbose' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ __metadata: { type: 'SP.RoleDefinition' }, Name: 'Contribute - no delete', Description: 'Add and edit list items; cannot delete items or versions (MDI).', Order: 200,
            BasePermissions: { __metadata: { type: 'SP.BasePermissions' }, High: String(c.BasePermissions.High), Low: low } }) });
        if (!r.ok) throw new Error('role ' + (await r.text()).slice(0, 200));
        rd = (await get("/web/roledefinitions?$filter=Name eq 'Contribute - no delete'&$select=Id")).value[0]; log('created permission level Contribute - no delete');
      }
      let ge = ((await get("/web/sitegroups?$filter=Title eq 'MDI Config Editors'&$select=Id")) || { value: [] }).value[0];
      if (!ge) { ge = await req('/web/sitegroups', { Title: 'MDI Config Editors', Description: 'Can edit MDI config lists. Managed from People.CanEditConfig (flow 03).' }); log('created group MDI Config Editors'); }
      const own = await get('/web/AssociatedOwnerGroup?$select=Id'), mem = await get('/web/AssociatedMemberGroup?$select=Id'), vis = await get('/web/AssociatedVisitorGroup?$select=Id');
      const RD = async n => (await get(`/web/roledefinitions/getbyname('${n}')?$select=Id`)).Id;
      const FULL = await RD('Full Control'), CONTRIB = await RD('Contribute'), READ = await RD('Read');
      const plan = [[PKG.perms.config, [[own.Id, FULL], [ge.Id, CONTRIB], [mem.Id, READ], [vis.Id, READ]]],
                    [PKG.perms.data,   [[own.Id, FULL], [ge.Id, rd.Id], [mem.Id, rd.Id], [vis.Id, READ]]],
                    [PKG.perms.log,    [[own.Id, FULL], [ge.Id, READ], [mem.Id, READ], [vis.Id, READ]]]];
      for (const [lists, grants] of plan) for (const l of lists) {
        try {
          const L = `/web/lists/getbytitle('${l}')`;
          await req(L + '/breakroleinheritance(copyRoleAssignments=false,clearSubscopes=true)');
          for (const [pid, rid] of grants) await req(L + `/roleassignments/addroleassignment(principalid=${pid},roledefid=${rid})`);
          log('permissions set', l);
        } catch (e) { console.warn('[MDI] permissions failed', l, e.message); }
      }
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[MDI] permissions step failed', e.message); }
  }
  try { const ex = await get("/web/lists/getbytitle('MDI_Config')/items?$select=ID&$filter=Title eq 'Site_URL_Live'");
        if (!(ex && ex.value && ex.value.length)) await req("/web/lists/getbytitle('MDI_Config')/items", { Title: 'Site_URL_Live', Kind: 'Site', SettingValue: SITE, Notes: 'Set by provisioner' }); } catch (e) {}
  // ---- SETUP CODE for the flow installer (tools/install_flows.js.txt) ----
  try {
    const ids = {};
    for (const l of ['People', 'MDI_Config', 'HxH_Shift', 'HxH_Hourly', 'Newspaper', 'Lines', 'Escalation_Contacts', 'Escalation_Log', 'TS_Photos'])
      ids[l] = (await get(`/web/lists/getbytitle('${l}')?$select=Id`)).Id;
    for (const l of ['SAF_Results']) { const x = await get(`/web/lists/getbytitle('${l}')?$select=Id`); if (x) ids[l] = x.Id; }   // optional modules
    const CODE = 'MDI1:' + btoa(JSON.stringify({ site: SITE, lists: ids }));
    window.MDI_SETUP_CODE = CODE;
    let copied = false;
    try { if (typeof copy === 'function') { copy(CODE); copied = true; } } catch (e) {}
    if (!copied) try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(CODE); copied = true; } catch (e) {}
    console.log('%cSETUP CODE for the flow installer' + (copied ? ' (already copied to your clipboard)' : ' (select the line below and copy it)'), 'font-weight:bold;color:#00B2A9');
    console.log(CODE);
  } catch (e) { console.warn('[MDI] could not build the SETUP CODE', e.message); }
  log('DONE. Next: tools/install_flows.js.txt (flows), then the apps (deploy/apps). Keep one IT site owner outside the People list. See README.');
})();
